self.state_dir.join(format!(\"{provider}.json\"));\n287\t\n288\t        // Open or create the bucket file.\n289\t        let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n290\t            .read(true)\n291\t            .write(true)\n292\t            .create(true)\n293\t            .truncate(false)\n294\t            .open(&bucket_path)\n295\t            .with_context(|| {\n296\t                format!(\n297\t                    \"failed to open rate limit bucket: {}\",\n298\t                    bucket_path.display()\n299\t                )\n300\t            })?;\n301\t\n302\t        // Exclusive lock for atomic read-modify-write.\n303\t        FileExt::lock_exclusive(&file).with_context(|| {\n304\t            format!(\n305\t                \"failed to acquire lock on rate limit bucket: {}\",\n306\t                bucket_path.display()\n307\t            )\n308\t        })?;\n309\t\n310\t        // Read current state.\n311\t        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&bucket_path).unwrap_or_default();\n312\t        let mut bucket: TokenBucket = if content.trim().is_empty() {\n313\t            TokenBucket::new(rpm)\n314\t        } else {\n315\t            serde_json::from_str(&content).unwrap_or_else(|_| TokenBucket::new(rpm))\n316\t        };\n317\t\n318\t        // Update capacity if config changed.\n319\t        bucket.capacity = rpm;\n320\t\n321\t        // Try to consume a token.\n322\t        let allowed = bucket.try_consume();\n323\t\n324\t        // Write updated state.\n325\t        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&bucket)\n326\t            .context(\"failed to serialize token bucket state\")?;\n327\t        std::fs::write(&bucket_path, &json).with_context(|| {\n328\t            format!(\n329\t                \"failed to write rate limit bucket: {}\",\n330\t                bucket_path.display()\n331\t            )\n332\t        })?;\n333\t\n334\t        // Release lock.\n335\t        FileExt::unlock(&file).with_context(|| {\n336\t            format!(\n337\t                \"failed to release lock on rate limit bucket: {}\",\n338\t                bucket_path.display()\n339\t            )\n340\t        })?;\n341\t\n342\t        Ok(allowed)\n343\t    }\n344\t\n345\t    /// Check system resource health (CPU load and memory).\n346\t    ///\n347\t    /// Emits structured telemetry events when thresholds are exceeded; does not\n348\t    /// block dispatch. Logs tracing warnings in addition to telemetry for\n349\t    /// operator visibility.\n350\t    pub fn check_system_resources(\n351\t        cpu_load_warn: f64,\n352\t        memory_free_warn_mb: u64,\n353\t        telemetry: &Telemetry,\n354\t    ) {\n355\t        // CPU load: read /proc/loadavg on Linux.\n356\t        if let Ok(loadavg) = std::fs::read_to_string(\"/proc/loadavg\") {\n357\t            if let Some(load_str) = loadavg.split_whitespace().next() {\n358\t                if let Ok(load) = load_str.parse::<f64>() {\n359\t                    // Normalize by number of CPUs.\n360\t                    let num_cpus = std::thread::available_parallelism()\n361\t                        .map(|n| n.get())\n362\t                        .unwrap_or(1);\n363\t                    let normalized = load / num_cpus as f64;\n364\t                    if normalized > cpu_load_warn {\n365\t                        tracing::warn!(\n366\t                            load_1min = %load_str,\n367\t                            normalized = %format!(\"{:.2}\", normalized),\n368\t                            threshold = %format!(\"{:.2}\", cpu_load_warn),\n369\t                            \"CPU load exceeds warning threshold\"\n370\t                        );\n371\t                        // Emit structured telemetry event.\n372\t                        let _ = telemetry.emit(EventKind::FleetCpuSaturated {\n373\t                            load_average: load,\n374\t                            threshold: cpu_load_warn,\n375\t                            core_count: num_cpus,\n376\t                        });\n377\t                    }\n378\t                }\n379\t            }\n380\t        }\n381\t\n382\t        // Memory: read /proc/meminfo on Linux.\n383\t        if let Ok(meminfo) = std::fs::read_to_string(\"/proc/meminfo\") {\n384\t            let mut mem_available_kb: Option<u64> = None;\n385\t            for line in meminfo.lines() {\n386\t                if line.starts_with(\"MemAvailable:\") {\n387\t                    if let Some(val) = line.split_whitespace().nth(1) {\n388\t                        mem_available_kb = val.parse().ok();\n389\t                    }\n390\t                    break;\n391\t                }\n392\t            }\n393\t            if let Some(avail_kb) = mem_available_kb {\n394\t                let avail_mb = avail_kb / 1024;\n395\t                if avail_mb < memory_free_warn_mb {\n396\t                    tracing::warn!(\n397\t                        available_mb = avail_mb,\n398\t                        threshold_mb = memory_free_warn_mb,\n399\t                        \"available memory below warning threshold\"\n400\t                    );\n401\t                    // Emit structured telemetry event.\n402\t                    let _ = telemetry.emit(EventKind::FleetMemoryLow {\n403\t                        free_mb: avail_mb,\n404\t                        threshold_mb: memory_free_warn_mb,\n405\t                    });\n406\t                }\n407\t            }\n408\t        }\n409\t    }\n410\t}\n411\t\n412\t// Provider/model concurrency limits and RPM rate limiting.\n//!\n//! Before dispatching an agent, the worker checks:\n//! 1. **Concurrency limits**: Are fewer than `max_concurrent` workers using\n//!    this provider/model? Checked via the worker registry.\n//! 2. **RPM limits**: Has the provider's requests-per-minute budget been\n//!    exceeded? Checked via a file-based token bucket.\n//!\n//! If either check fails, the caller should back off and retry.\n//!\n//! Depends on: `config`, `registry`, `types`.\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse fs2::FileExt;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\nuse crate::config::LimitsConfig;\nuse crate::registry::Registry;\nuse crate::telemetry::{EventKind, Telemetry};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// RateLimitDecision\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Result of a rate limit check.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]\npub enum RateLimitDecision {\n    /// Dispatch is allowed.\n    Allowed,\n    /// Provider concurrency limit reached.\n    ProviderConcurrencyExceeded {\n        provider: String,\n        current: u32,\n        limit: u32,\n    },\n    /// Model concurrency limit reached.\n    ModelConcurrencyExceeded {\n        model: String,\n        current: u32,\n        limit: u32,\n    },\n    /// RPM limit reached for this provider.\n    RpmExceeded {\n        provider: String,\n        requests_per_minute: u32,\n    },\n}\n\nimpl RateLimitDecision {\n    /// Whether dispatch is allowed.\n    pub fn is_allowed(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, RateLimitDecision::Allowed)\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::fmt::Display for RateLimitDecision {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            RateLimitDecision::Allowed => write!(f, \"allowed\"),\n            RateLimitDecision::ProviderConcurrencyExceeded {\n                provider,\n                current,\n                limit,\n            } => write!(\n                f,\n                \"provider '{}' concurrency exceeded ({}/{})\",\n                provider, current, limit\n            ),\n            RateLimitDecision::ModelConcurrencyExceeded {\n                model,\n                current,\n                limit,\n            } => write!(\n                f,\n                \"model '{}' concurrency exceeded ({}/{})\",\n                model, current, limit\n            ),\n            RateLimitDecision::RpmExceeded {\n                provider,\n                requests_per_minute,\n            } => write!(\n                f,\n                \"provider '{}' RPM limit exceeded ({}/min)\",\n                provider, requests_per_minute\n            ),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Token Bucket (file-based)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// On-disk token bucket state for RPM limiting.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct TokenBucket {\n    /// Current available tokens (fractional for smooth refill).\n    pub tokens: f64,\n    /// Maximum tokens (= requests_per_minute).\n    pub capacity: u32,\n    /// Last time tokens were refilled.\n    pub last_refill: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\nimpl TokenBucket {\n    pub(crate) fn new(capacity: u32) -> Self {\n        TokenBucket {\n            tokens: capacity as f64,\n            capacity,\n            last_refill: Utc::now(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Refill tokens based on elapsed time since last refill.\n    fn refill(&mut self) {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let elapsed_secs = (now - self.last_refill).num_milliseconds().max(0) as f64 / 1000.0;\n        let refill_rate = self.capacity as f64 / 60.0; // tokens per second\n        self.tokens = (self.tokens + elapsed_secs * refill_rate).min(self.capacity as f64);\n        self.last_refill = now;\n    }\n\n    /// Try to consume one token. Returns true if successful.\n    fn try_consume(&mut self) -> bool {\n        self.refill();\n        if self.tokens >= 1.0 {\n            self.tokens -= 1.0;\n            true\n        } else {\n            false\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// RateLimiter\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Enforces provider/model concurrency limits and RPM rate limiting.\npub struct RateLimiter {\n    config: LimitsConfig,\n    /// Directory for token bucket state files (`~/.needle/state/rate_limits/`).\n    state_dir: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl RateLimiter {\n    /// Create a rate limiter with the given config and state directory.\n    pub fn new(config: LimitsConfig, state_dir: &Path) -> Self {\n        RateLimiter {\n            config,\n            state_dir: state_dir.join(\"rate_limits\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Check all rate limits before dispatching.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `Allowed` if dispatch can proceed, or a specific reason why not.\n    /// The caller should back off and retry if not allowed.\n    pub fn check(\n        &self,\n        provider: Option<&str>,\n        model: Option<&str>,\n        registry: &Registry,\n    ) -> Result<RateLimitDecision> {\n        // Check provider concurrency.\n        if let Some(provider_name) = provider {\n            if let Some(provider_limits) = self.config.providers.get(provider_name) {\n                if let Some(max_concurrent) = provider_limits.max_concurrent {\n                    let decision =\n                        self.check_provider_concurrency(provider_name, max_concurrent, registry)?;\n                    if !decision.is_allowed() {\n                        return Ok(decision);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check model concurrency.\n        if let Some(model_name) = model {\n            if let Some(model_limits) = self.config.models.get(model_name) {\n                if let Some(max_concurrent) = model_limits.max_concurrent {\n                    let decision =\n                        self.check_model_concurrency(model_name, max_concurrent, registry)?;\n                    if !decision.is_allowed() {\n                        return Ok(decision);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check RPM.\n        if let Some(provider_name) = provider {\n            if let Some(provider_limits) = self.config.providers.get(provider_name) {\n                if let Some(rpm) = provider_limits.requests_per_minute {\n                    let decision = self.check_rpm(provider_name, rpm)?;\n                    if !decision.is_allowed() {\n                        return Ok(decision);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(RateLimitDecision::Allowed)\n    }\n\n    /// Check provider concurrency against the worker registry.\n    fn check_provider_concurrency(\n        &self,\n        provider: &str,\n        max_concurrent: u32,\n        registry: &Registry,\n    ) -> Result<RateLimitDecision> {\n        let workers = registry.list()?;\n        let active_count = workers\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|w| w.provider.as_deref() == Some(provider))\n            .count() as u32;\n\n        if active_count >= max_concurrent {\n            Ok(RateLimitDecision::ProviderConcurrencyExceeded {\n                provider: provider.to_string(),\n                current: active_count,\n                limit: max_concurrent,\n            })\n        } else {\n            Ok(RateLimitDecision::Allowed)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Check model concurrency against the worker registry.\n    fn check_model_concurrency(\n        &self,\n        model: &str,\n        max_concurrent: u32,\n        registry: &Registry,\n    ) -> Result<RateLimitDecision> {\n        let workers = registry.list()?;\n        let active_count = workers\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|w| w.model.as_deref() == Some(model))\n            .count() as u32;\n\n        if active_count >= max_concurrent {\n            Ok(RateLimitDecision::ModelConcurrencyExceeded {\n                model: model.to_string(),\n                current: active_count,\n                limit: max_concurrent,\n            })\n        } else {\n            Ok(RateLimitDecision::Allowed)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Check RPM via a file-based token bucket.\n    fn check_rpm(&self, provider: &str, rpm: u32) -> Result<RateLimitDecision> {\n        if rpm == 0 {\n            return Ok(RateLimitDecision::Allowed);\n        }\n\n        let allowed = self.try_acquire_rpm_token(provider, rpm)?;\n        if allowed {\n            Ok(RateLimitDecision::Allowed)\n        } else {\n            Ok(RateLimitDecision::RpmExceeded {\n                provider: provider.to_string(),\n                requests_per_minute: rpm,\n            })\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Try to acquire an RPM token from the file-based token bucket.\n    ///\n    /// Uses flock for cross-process safety.\n    fn try_acquire_rpm_token(&self, provider: &str, rpm: u32) -> Result<bool> {\n        // Ensure the rate_limits directory exists.\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(&self.state_dir).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to create rate_limits directory: {}\",\n                self.state_dir.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        let bucket_path = self.state_dir.join(format!(\"{provider}.json\"));\n\n        // Open or create the bucket file.\n        let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n            .read(true)\n            .write(true)\n            .create(true)\n            .truncate(false)\n            .open(&bucket_path)\n            .with_context(|| {\n                format!(\n                    \"failed to open rate limit bucket: {}\",\n                    bucket_path.display()\n                )\n            })?;\n\n        // Exclusive lock for atomic read-modify-write.\n        FileExt::lock_exclusive(&file).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to acquire lock on rate limit bucket: {}\",\n                bucket_path.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        // Read current state.\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&bucket_path).unwrap_or_default();\n        let mut bucket: TokenBucket = if content.trim().is_empty() {\n            TokenBucket::new(rpm)\n        } else {\n            serde_json::from_str(&content).unwrap_or_else(|_| TokenBucket::new(rpm))\n        };\n\n        // Update capacity if config changed.\n        bucket.capacity = rpm;\n\n        // Try to consume a token.\n        let allowed = bucket.try_consume();\n\n        // Write updated state.\n        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&bucket)\n            .context(\"failed to serialize token bucket state\")?;\n        std::fs::write(&bucket_path, &json).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to write rate limit bucket: {}\",\n                bucket_path.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        // Release lock.\n        FileExt::unlock(&file).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to release lock on rate limit bucket: {}\",\n                bucket_path.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        Ok(allowed)\n    }\n\n    /// Check system resource health (CPU load and memory).\n    ///\n    /// Emits structured telemetry events when thresholds are exceeded; does not\n    /// block dispatch. Logs tracing warnings in addition to telemetry for\n    /// operator visibility.\n    pub fn check_system_resources(\n        cpu_load_warn: f64,\n        memory_free_warn_mb: u64,\n        telemetry: &Telemetry,\n    ) {\n        // CPU load: read /proc/loadavg on Linux.\n        if let Ok(loadavg) = std::fs::read_to_string(\"/proc/loadavg\") {\n            if let Some(load_str) = loadavg.split_whitespace().next() {\n                if let Ok(load) = load_str.parse::<f64>() {\n                    // Normalize by number of CPUs.\n                    let num_cpus = std::thread::available_parallelism()\n                        .map(|n| n.get())\n                        .unwrap_or(1);\n                    let normalized = load / num_cpus as f64;\n                    if normalized > cpu_load_warn {\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            load_1min = %load_str,\n                            normalized = %format!(\"{:.2}\", normalized),\n                            threshold = %format!(\"{:.2}\", cpu_load_warn),\n                            \"CPU load exceeds warning threshold\"\n                        );\n                        // Emit structured telemetry event.\n                        let _ = telemetry.emit(EventKind::FleetCpuSaturated {\n                            load_average: load,\n                            threshold: cpu_load_warn,\n                            core_count: num_cpus,\n                        });\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Memory: read /proc/meminfo on Linux.\n        if let Ok(meminfo) = std::fs::read_to_string(\"/proc/meminfo\") {\n            let mut mem_available_kb: Option<u64> = None;\n            for line in meminfo.lines() {\n                if line.starts_with(\"MemAvailable:\") {\n                    if let Some(val) = line.split_whitespace().nth(1) {\n                        mem_available_kb = val.parse().ok();\n                    }\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            if let Some(avail_kb) = mem_available_kb {\n                let avail_mb = avail_kb / 1024;\n                if avail_mb < memory_free_warn_mb {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        available_mb = avail_mb,\n                        threshold_mb = memory_free_warn_mb,\n                        \"available memory below warning threshold\"\n                    );\n                    // Emit structured telemetry event.\n                    let _ = telemetry.emit(EventKind::FleetMemoryLow {\n                        free_mb: avail_mb,\n                        threshold_mb: memory_free_warn_mb,\n                    });\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// Structured telemetry — JSONL event stream, never on stdout/stderr.\n//!\n//! Every state transition, claim attempt, dispatch, and outcome emits a typed\n//! event. The emitter is non-blocking: events are queued and written by a\n//! background task. A broken sink never blocks or panics the worker.\n//!\n//! ## Architecture\n//! ```text\n//! worker → emit() → mpsc::Sender → [background task] → TelemetryBus → Vec<Box<dyn Sink>>\n//! ```\n//!\n//! Depends on: `types`.\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};\nuse std::sync::Arc;\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse tokio::sync::mpsc;\n\nuse crate::config::{ColorMode, HookConfig, StdoutFormat, StdoutSinkConfig, TelemetryConfig};\nuse crate::types::{BeadId, WorkerId, WorkerState};\n\n// ─── OTLP Sink (feature-gated) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"otlp\")]\npub mod otlp;\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"otlp\")]\npub use otlp::{create_tracing_layer, OtlpSink};\n\n// ─── Test Utilities ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\npub mod test_utils;\n\n// ─── TelemetryEvent ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A single structured telemetry record.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct TelemetryEvent {\n    /// UTC timestamp with millisecond precision.\n    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,\n    /// Discriminant string, e.g. `\"state_transition\"`.\n    pub event_type: String,\n    /// The worker that emitted this event.\n    pub worker_id: WorkerId,\n    /// Session identifier (8 hex chars, unique per worker boot).\n    pub session_id: String,\n    /// Monotonically increasing counter within the session.\n    pub sequence: u64,\n    /// Bead context when relevant.\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub bead_id: Option<BeadId>,\n    /// Workspace directory when relevant.\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n    /// Event-specific payload as a JSON value.\n    pub data: serde_json::Value,\n    /// Optional duration in milliseconds for timed operations.\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub duration_ms: Option<u64>,\n    /// W3C trace-id hex (32 lowercase hex chars). Present only when emitted inside an OTel span.\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub trace_id: Option<String>,\n    /// W3C span-id hex (16 lowercase hex chars).
