EventKind::MendDependencyCleaned { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDependencyRemoved { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::EffortRecorded { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::MitosisEvaluated { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::VerificationFailed { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::VerificationPassed { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::UnravelAnalyzed { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::UnravelSkipped { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::OutputTransformSpawned { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::OutputTransformExited { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::OutputTransformSkipped { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformStarted { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformCompleted { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformFailed { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformSkipped { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectDecisionExtracted { bead_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectAdrCreated { bead_id, .. } => Some(bead_id.clone()),\n            EventKind::MitosisSplit { parent_id, .. }\n            | EventKind::MitosisSkipped { parent_id, .. } => Some(parent_id.clone()),\n            EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted { bead_id, .. } => bead_id.clone(),\n            EventKind::WorkerBooting { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerStopped { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerErrored { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerExhausted { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerIdle { .. }\n            | EventKind::IdleSleepCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::IdleSleepEntered { .. }\n            | EventKind::StateTransition { .. }\n            | EventKind::InitStepStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::InitStepCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { .. }\n            | EventKind::StrandEvaluated { .. }\n            | EventKind::StrandSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::QueueEmpty\n            | EventKind::HealthCheck { .. }\n            | EventKind::FleetCpuSaturated { .. }\n            | EventKind::FleetMemoryLow { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendOrphanedLockRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDbRepaired { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDbRebuilt\n            | EventKind::MendCycleSummary { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendIdleWorkerFlagged { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendWorkerDeregistered { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendOrphanedHeartbeatRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendBeadReleaseFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDependencyCleanupFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendLockRemoveFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendRateLimitCleaned { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendRateLimitProviderRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendRateLimitProviderReset { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendZeroActivityLogCleaned { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendTraceCleanup { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendLearningCleanup { .. }\n            | EventKind::BudgetWarning { .. }\n            | EventKind::BudgetStop { .. }\n            | EventKind::RateLimitWait { .. }\n            | EventKind::RateLimitAllowed { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectConsolidated { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectTranscriptsRead { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectDriftDetected { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectDriftPromoted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectLearningPromoted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectLearningDeduplicated { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectClaudeMdWritten { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseScannerStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseScannerCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseScannerFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::UpgradeDetected { .. }\n            | EventKind::UpgradeCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::RollbackCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanaryStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanarySuiteCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanaryPromoted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanaryRejected { .. }\n            | EventKind::ClaimRaceLostSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::SinkError { .. }\n            | EventKind::OtlpDropped { .. }\n            | EventKind::OtlpShutdownTimeout { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftDetectionStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftDetectionCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftDetectionSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftReportWritten { .. }\n            | EventKind::DecisionDetectionStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DecisionDetectionCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DecisionDetectionSkipped { .. } => None,\n            EventKind::PulseBeadCreated { bead_id, .. } => Some(bead_id.clone()),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Serialize event-specific payload to a JSON value.\n    pub fn to_data(&self) -> serde_json::Value {\n        match self {\n            EventKind::WorkerBooting {\n                worker_name,\n                version,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"worker_name\": worker_name, \"version\": version })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerStarted {\n                worker_name,\n                version,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"worker_name\": worker_name, \"version\": version })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerStopped {\n                reason,\n                beads_processed,\n                uptime_secs,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"reason\": reason,\n                    \"beads_processed\": beads_processed,\n                    \"uptime_secs\": uptime_secs,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerErrored {\n                error_type,\n                error_message,\n                beads_processed,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"error_type\": error_type,\n                    \"error_message\": error_message,\n                    \"beads_processed\": beads_processed,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerExhausted {\n                cycle_count,\n                last_strand,\n                waterfall_restarts,\n                restart_triggers,\n                strand_evaluations,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"cycle_count\": cycle_count,\n                    \"last_strand_evaluated\": last_strand,\n                    \"waterfall_restarts\": waterfall_restarts,\n                    \"restart_triggers\": restart_triggers,\n                    \"strand_evaluations\": strand_evaluations,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerIdle { backoff_seconds } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"backoff_seconds\": backoff_seconds })\n            }\n            EventKind::StateTransition { from, to } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"from\": format!(\"{from}\"), \"to\": format!(\"{to}\") })\n            }\n            EventKind::InitStepStarted { step } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"step\": step })\n            }\n            EventKind::InitStepCompleted { step, duration_ms } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"step\": step, \"duration_ms\": duration_ms })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { elapsed_ms } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"elapsed_ms\": elapsed_ms })\n            }\n            EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n                strand_name,\n                result,\n                duration_ms,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"strand_name\": strand_name,\n                    \"result\": result,\n                    \"duration_ms\": duration_ms,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::StrandSkipped {\n                strand_name,\n                reason,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"strand_name\": strand_name, \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::QueueEmpty => serde_json::json!({}),\n            EventKind::ClaimAttempt { bead_id, attempt } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"attempt\": attempt })\n            }\n            EventKind::ClaimSuccess {\n                bead_id,\n                priority,\n                strand,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"priority\": priority,\n                    \"strand\": strand,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ClaimRaceLost { bead_id } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref() })\n            }\n            EventKind::ClaimRaceLostSkipped {\n                consecutive_losses,\n                threshold,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"consecutive_losses\": consecutive_losses,\n                    \"threshold\": threshold,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ClaimFailed { bead_id, reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::BeadReleased { bead_id, reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::BeadReleaseFailed { bead_id, reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::BeadCompleted {\n                bead_id,\n                duration_ms,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"duration_ms\": duration_ms })\n            }\n            EventKind::BeadOrphaned { bead_id } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref() })\n            }\n            EventKind::DispatchStarted {\n                bead_id,\n                agent,\n                prompt_len,\n                template_name,\n                template_version,\n                prompt_hash,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"agent\": agent,\n                    \"prompt_len\": prompt_len,\n                    \"template_name\": template_name,\n                    \"template_version\": template_version,\n                    \"prompt_hash\": format!(\"sha256:{prompt_hash}\"),\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::DispatchCompleted {\n                bead_id,\n                exit_code,\n                duration_ms,\n                agent,\n                model,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"exit_code\": exit_code,\n                    \"duration_ms\": duration_ms,\n                    \"agent\": agent,\n                    \"model\": model,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::BuildTimeout {\n                bead_id,\n                timeout_secs,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"timeout_secs\": timeout_secs,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::BuildHeartbeat {\n                bead_id,\n                elapsed_ms,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"elapsed_ms\": elapsed_ms,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::OutcomeClassified {\n                bead_id,\n                outcome,\n                exit_code,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"outcome\": outcome,\n                    \"exit_code\": exit_code,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::OutcomeHandled {\n                bead_id,\n                outcome,\n                action,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"outcome\": outcome,\n                    \"action\": action,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                bead_id,\n                outcome,\n                operation,\n                error,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"outcome\": outcome,\n                    \"operation\": operation,\n                    \"error\": error,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted { bead_id, state } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref().map(|b| b.as_ref()),\n                    \"state\": state,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::StuckDetected { bead_id, age_secs } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"age_secs\": age_secs })\n            }\n            EventKind::StuckReleased {\n                bead_id,\n                peer_worker,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(), \"peer_worker\": peer_worker })\n            }\n            EventKind::HealthCheck {\n                db_healthy,\n                disk_free_mb,\n                peer_count,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"db_healthy\": db_healthy,\n                    \"disk_free_mb\": disk_free_mb,\n                    \"peer_count\": peer_count,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::FleetCpuSaturated {\n                load_average,\n                threshold,\n                core_count,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"load_average\": load_average,\n                    \"threshold\": threshold,\n                    \"core_count\": core_count,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::FleetMemoryLow {\n                free_mb,\n                threshold_mb,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"free_mb\": free_mb,\n                    \"threshold_mb\": threshold_mb,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendOrphanedLockRemoved {\n                lock_path,\n                age_secs,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"lock_path\": lock_path, \"age_secs\": age_secs })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendDependencyCleaned {\n                bead_id,\n                blocker_id,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"blocker_id\": blocker_id.as_ref(),\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendDbRepaired { warnings, fixed } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"warnings\": warnings, \"fixed\": fixed })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendDbRebuilt => serde_json::json!({}),\n            EventKind::MendCycleSummary {\n                beads_released,\n                locks_removed,\n                deps_cleaned,\n                db_repaired,\n                db_rebuilt,\n                agent_logs_cleaned,\n                zero_activity_logs_cleaned,\n                traces_pruned,\n                traces_deleted,\n                workers_deregistered,\n                idle_workers_flagged,\n                rate_limits_cleaned,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"beads_released\": beads_released,\n                    \"locks_removed\": locks_removed,\n                    \"deps_cleaned\": deps_cleaned,\n                    \"db_repaired\": db_repaired,\n                    \"db_rebuilt\": db_rebuilt,\n                    \"agent_logs_cleaned\": agent_logs_cleaned,\n                    \"zero_activity_logs_cleaned\": zero_activity_logs_cleaned,\n                    \"traces_pruned\": traces_pruned,\n                    \"traces_deleted\": traces_deleted,\n                    \"workers_deregistered\": workers_deregistered,\n                    \"idle_workers_flagged\": idle_workers_flagged,\n                    \"rate_limits_cleaned\": rate_limits_cleaned,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::EffortRecorded {\n                bead_id,\n                elapsed_ms,\n                agent_name,\n                model,\n                tokens_in,\n                tokens_out,\n                estimated_cost_usd,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"elapsed_ms\": elapsed_ms,\n                    \"agent_name\": agent_name,\n                    \"model\": model,\n                    \"tokens_in\": tokens_in,\n                    \"tokens_out\": tokens_out,\n                    \"estimated_cost_usd\": estimated_cost_usd,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::BudgetWarning {\n                daily_cost,\n                threshold,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"daily_cost\": daily_cost,\n                    \"threshold\": threshold,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::BudgetStop {\n                daily_cost,\n                threshold,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"daily_cost\": daily_cost,\n                    \"threshold\": threshold,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::RateLimitWait {\n                provider,\n                model,\n                reason,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"provider\": provider,\n                    \"model\": model,\n                    \"reason\": reason,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::RateLimitAllowed { provider, model } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"provider\": provider,\n                    \"model\": model,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n                bead_id,\n                splittable,\n                proposed_children,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"splittable\": splittable,\n                    \"proposed_children\": proposed_children,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MitosisSplit {\n                parent_id,\n                children_created,\n                children_skipped,\n                child_ids,\n            } => {\n                let ids: Vec<&str> = child_ids.iter().map(|id| id.as_ref()).collect();\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"parent_id\": parent_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"children_created\": children_created,\n                    \"children_skipped\": children_skipped,\n                    \"child_ids\": ids,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MitosisSkipped {\n                parent_id,\n                existing_children,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"parent_id\": parent_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"existing_children\": existing_children,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::VerificationFailed {\n                bead_id,\n                command,\n                exit_code,\n                output,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"command\": command,\n                    \"exit_code\": exit_code,\n                    \"output\": output,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::VerificationPassed { bead_id, gates_run } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"gates_run\": gates_run,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::UnravelAnalyzed {\n                bead_id,\n                alternatives_proposed,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"alternatives_proposed\": alternatives_proposed,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::UnravelSkipped { bead_id, reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"reason\": reason,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::PulseScannerStarted { scanner_name } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"scanner_name\": scanner_name })\n            }\n            EventKind::PulseScannerCompleted {\n                scanner_name,\n                findings_count,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"scanner_name\": scanner_name,\n                    \"findings_count\": findings_count,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::PulseScannerFailed {\n                scanner_name,\n                error,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"scanner_name\": scanner_name,\n                    \"error\": error,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::PulseBeadCreated {\n                bead_id,\n                scanner_name,\n                severity,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.as_ref(),\n                    \"scanner_name\": scanner_name,\n                    \"severity\": severity,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::PulseSkipped { reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::UpgradeDetected { old_hash, new_hash } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"old_hash\": old_hash, \"new_hash\": new_hash })\n            }\n            EventKind::UpgradeCompleted { new_hash } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"new_hash\": new_hash })\n            }\n            EventKind::RollbackCompleted {\n                rolled_back_hash,\n                restored_hash,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"rolled_back_hash\": rolled_back_hash,\n                    \"restored_hash\": restored_hash,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::OutputTransformSpawned {\n                bead_id,\n                transform_cmd,\n                log_path,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                \"transform_cmd\": transform_cmd,\n                \"log_path\": log_path,\n            }),\n            EventKind::OutputTransformExited { bead_id, exit_code } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id, \"exit_code\": exit_code })\n            }\n            EventKind::OutputTransformSkipped { bead_id, reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id, \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::TransformStarted {\n                bead_id,\n                transform_binary,\n                agent,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                \"transform_binary\": transform_binary,\n                \"agent\": agent,\n            }),\n            EventKind::TransformCompleted {\n                bead_id,\n                events_written,\n                duration_ms,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                \"events_written\": events_written,\n                \"duration_ms\": duration_ms,\n            }),\n            EventKind::TransformFailed {\n                bead_id,\n                error,\n                exit_code,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                \"error\": error,\n                \"exit_code\": exit_code,\n            }),\n            EventKind::TransformSkipped { bead_id, reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"bead_id\": bead_id, \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendTraceCleanup {\n                traces_pruned,\n                traces_deleted,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"traces_pruned\": traces_pruned,\n                    \"traces_deleted\": traces_deleted,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendLearningCleanup {\n                pruned,\n                consolidated,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"pruned\": pruned,\n                    \"consolidated\": consolidated,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendIdleWorkerFlagged {\n                worker_id,\n                pid,\n                age_secs,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"worker_id\": worker_id,\n                    \"pid\": pid,\n                    \"age_secs\": age_secs,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendWorkerDeregistered { worker_id, pid } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"worker_id\": worker_id,\n                    \"pid\": pid,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendOrphanedHeartbeatRemoved {\n                worker_id,\n                age_secs,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"worker_id\": worker_id,\n                    \"age_secs\": age_secs,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendDependencyRemoved {\n                bead_id,\n                blocker_id,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                    \"blocker_id\": blocker_id,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendBeadReleaseFailed {\n                bead_id,\n                assignee,\n                error,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                    \"assignee\": assignee,\n                    \"error\": error,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendDependencyCleanupFailed {\n                bead_id,\n                blocker_id,\n                error,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                    \"blocker_id\": blocker_id,\n                    \"error\": error,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendLockRemoveFailed { lock_path, error } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"lock_path\": lock_path,\n                    \"error\": error,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendRateLimitCleaned { provider, age_secs } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"provider\": provider,\n                    \"age_secs\": age_secs,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendRateLimitProviderRemoved { provider } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"provider\": provider })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendRateLimitProviderReset { provider, age_secs } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"provider\": provider,\n                    \"age_secs\": age_secs,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::MendZeroActivityLogCleaned {\n                worker_id,\n                log_path,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"worker_id\": worker_id,\n                    \"log_path\": log_path,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::CanaryStarted { suite } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"suite\": suite })\n            }\n            EventKind::CanarySuiteCompleted {\n                suite,\n                passed,\n                failed,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"suite\": suite,\n                    \"passed\": passed,\n                    \"failed\": failed,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::CanaryPromoted { hash } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"hash\": hash })\n            }\n            EventKind::CanaryRejected { reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectStarted { beads_since_last } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"beads_since_last\": beads_since_last })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectConsolidated {\n                learnings_added,\n                learnings_pruned,\n                skills_promoted,\n                beads_processed,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"learnings_added\": learnings_added,\n                    \"learnings_pruned\": learnings_pruned,\n                    \"skills_promoted\": skills_promoted,\n                    \"beads_processed\": beads_processed,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectSkipped { reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectTranscriptsRead {\n                sessions_count,\n                entries_count,\n                parse_errors,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"sessions_count\": sessions_count,\n                    \"entries_count\": entries_count,\n                    \"parse_errors\": parse_errors,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectDriftDetected {\n                cluster_size,\n                category,\n                sessions,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"cluster_size\": cluster_size,\n                    \"category\": category,\n                    \"sessions\": sessions,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectDriftPromoted { pattern, category } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"pattern\": pattern,\n                    \"category\": category,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectDecisionExtracted {\n                bead_id,\n                has_alternatives,\n                rationale_length,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.to_string(),\n                    \"has_alternatives\": has_alternatives,\n                    \"rationale_length\": rationale_length,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectAdrCreated { bead_id, path } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"bead_id\": bead_id.to_string(),\n                    \"path\": path,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectLearningPromoted {\n                learning_id,\n                target_path,\n                workspace_count,\n                is_decision,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"learning_id\": learning_id,\n                    \"target_path\": target_path,\n                    \"workspace_count\": workspace_count,\n                    \"is_decision\": is_decision,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectLearningDeduplicated {\n                learning_id,\n                existing_entry,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"learning_id\": learning_id,\n                    \"existing_entry\": existing_entry,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::ReflectClaudeMdWritten {\n                path,\n                entries_added,\n                entries_updated,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"path\": path,\n                    \"entries_added\": entries_added,\n                    \"entries_updated\": entries_updated,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::DriftDetectionStarted { sessions_analyzed } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"sessions_analyzed\": sessions_analyzed })\n            }\n            EventKind::DriftDetectionCompleted {\n                sessions_analyzed,\n                clusters_found,\n                evolved_count,\n                inconsistent_count,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"sessions_analyzed\": sessions_analyzed,\n                    \"clusters_found\": clusters_found,\n                    \"evolved_count\": evolved_count,\n                    \"inconsistent_count\": inconsistent_count,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::DriftDetectionSkipped { reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::DriftReportWritten {\n                report_path,\n                clusters,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"report_path\": report_path,\n                    \"clusters\": clusters,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::DecisionDetectionStarted { sessions_analyzed } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"sessions_analyzed\": sessions_analyzed })\n            }\n            EventKind::DecisionDetectionCompleted {\n                sessions_analyzed,\n                decisions_found,\n            } => {\n                serde_json::json!({\n                    \"sessions_analyzed\": sessions_analyzed,\n                    \"decisions_found\": decisions_found,\n                })\n            }\n            EventKind::DecisionDetectionSkipped { reason } => {\n                serde_json::json!({ \"reason\": reason })\n            }\n            EventKind::SinkError { message } => serde_json::json!({ \"message\": message }),\n            EventKind::OtlpDropped {\n                signal,\n                dropped_count,\n                queue_full,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"signal\": signal,\n                \"dropped_count\": dropped_count,\n                \"queue_full\": queue_full,\n            }),\n            EventKind::OtlpShutdownTimeout {\n                flushed_batches,\n                remaining_batches,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"flushed_batches\": flushed_batches,\n                \"remaining_batches\": remaining_batches,\n            }),\n            EventKind::IdleSleepCompleted {\n                backoff_secs,\n                elapsed_secs,\n                shutdown_checks,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"backoff_secs\": backoff_secs,\n                \"elapsed_secs\": elapsed_secs,\n                \"shutdown_checks\": shutdown_checks,\n            }),\n            EventKind::IdleSleepEntered {\n                backoff_secs,\n                beads_processed,\n                uptime_secs,\n            } => serde_json::json!({\n                \"backoff_secs\": backoff_secs,\n                \"beads_processed\": beads_processed,\n                \"uptime_secs\": uptime_secs,\n            }),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Extract duration_ms from events that carry it.\n    pub fn duration_ms(&self) -> Option<u64> {\n        match self {\n            EventKind::DispatchCompleted { duration_ms, .. }\n            | EventKind::BeadCompleted { duration_ms, .. }\n            | EventKind::StrandEvaluated { duration_ms, .. }\n            | EventKind::InitStepCompleted { duration_ms, .. }\n            | EventKind::EffortRecorded {\n                elapsed_ms: duration_ms,\n                ..\n            } => Some(*duration_ms),\n            EventKind::WorkerBooting { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerStopped { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerErrored { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerExhausted { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerIdle { .. }\n            | EventKind::StateTransition { .. }\n            | EventKind::InitStepStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { .. }\n            | EventKind::StrandSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::QueueEmpty\n            | EventKind::ClaimAttempt { .. }\n            | EventKind::ClaimSuccess { .. }\n            | EventKind::ClaimRaceLost { .. }\n            | EventKind::ClaimRaceLostSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::ClaimFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }\n            | EventKind::BeadReleaseFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::BeadOrphaned { .. }\n            | EventKind::DispatchStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::BuildTimeout { .. }\n            | EventKind::BuildHeartbeat { .. }\n            | EventKind::OutcomeClassified { .. }\n            | EventKind::OutcomeHandled { .. }\n            | EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout { .. }\n            | EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted { .. }\n            | EventKind::StuckDetected { .. }\n            | EventKind::StuckReleased { .. }\n            | EventKind::HealthCheck { .. }\n            | EventKind::FleetCpuSaturated { .. }\n            | EventKind::FleetMemoryLow { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendOrphanedLockRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDependencyCleaned { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDbRepaired { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDbRebuilt\n            | EventKind::MendCycleSummary { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendIdleWorkerFlagged { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendWorkerDeregistered { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendOrphanedHeartbeatRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDependencyRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendBeadReleaseFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendDependencyCleanupFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendLockRemoveFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendRateLimitCleaned { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendRateLimitProviderRemoved { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendRateLimitProviderReset { .. }\n            | EventKind::BudgetWarning { .. }\n            | EventKind::BudgetStop { .. }\n            | EventKind::RateLimitWait { .. }\n            | EventKind::RateLimitAllowed { .. }\n            | EventKind::MitosisEvaluated { .. }\n            | EventKind::MitosisSplit { .. }\n            | EventKind::MitosisSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::VerificationFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::VerificationPassed { .. }\n            | EventKind::UnravelAnalyzed { .. }\n            | EventKind::UnravelSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseScannerStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseScannerCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseScannerFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseBeadCreated { .. }\n            | EventKind::PulseSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::UpgradeDetected { .. }\n            | EventKind::UpgradeCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::RollbackCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendTraceCleanup { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendLearningCleanup { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanaryStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanarySuiteCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanaryPromoted { .. }\n            | EventKind::CanaryRejected { .. }\n            | EventKind::OutputTransformSpawned { .. }\n            | EventKind::OutputTransformExited { .. }\n            | EventKind::OutputTransformSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformFailed { .. }\n            | EventKind::TransformSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectConsolidated { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectTranscriptsRead { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectDriftDetected { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectDriftPromoted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectDecisionExtracted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectAdrCreated { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectLearningPromoted { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectLearningDeduplicated { .. }\n            | EventKind::ReflectClaudeMdWritten { .. }\n            | EventKind::MendZeroActivityLogCleaned { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftDetectionStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftDetectionCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftDetectionSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::DriftReportWritten { .. }\n            | EventKind::DecisionDetectionStarted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DecisionDetectionCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::DecisionDetectionSkipped { .. }\n            | EventKind::SinkError { .. }\n            | EventKind::OtlpDropped { .. }\n            | EventKind::OtlpShutdownTimeout { .. }\n            | EventKind::IdleSleepCompleted { .. }\n            | EventKind::IdleSleepEntered { .. } => None,\n            EventKind::TransformCompleted { duration_ms, .. } => Some(*duration_ms),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── Sink trait ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Pluggable output backend for telemetry events.\n///\n/// Implement this trait to add a new sink — register an instance in the\n/// `TelemetryBus` and events fan out automatically, with no special-casing.\npub trait Sink: Send + Sync {\n    /// Accept a single event. Must not block indefinitely.\n    fn accept(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()>;\n\n    /// Flush any buffered state before shutdown.\n    ///\n    /// Implementations should respect `deadline`: if flushing cannot complete\n    /// within the duration, return an error rather than blocking indefinitely.\n    fn flush(&self, deadline: std::time::Duration) -> Result<()>;\n}\n\n/// Blanket impl for Arc-wrapped sinks.\n/// This enables Arc<Sink> to be used wherever Sink is required.\nimpl<T: Sink + ?Sized> Sink for Arc<T> {\n    fn accept(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n        self.as_ref().accept(event)\n    }\n\n    fn flush(&self, deadline: std::time::Duration) -> Result<()> {\n        self.as_ref().flush(deadline)\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── FileSink ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Writes JSONL telemetry to `<log_dir>/<worker>-<session>.jsonl`.\n///\n/// Append-only, one line per event. The log directory is created if it\n/// does not exist.\npub struct FileSink {\n    path: PathBuf,\n    writer: std::sync::Mutex<std::io::BufWriter<std::fs::File>>,\n}\n\nimpl FileSink {\n    /// Construct a sink using the default log directory (`~/.needle/logs/`).\n    pub fn new(worker_id: &str, session_id: &str) -> Result<Self> {\n        let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \".\".to_string());\n        let log_dir = PathBuf::from(&home).join(\".needle\").join(\"logs\");\n        Self::with_dir(&log_dir, worker_id, session_id)\n    }\n\n    /// Construct a sink writing to a specific directory.\n    ///\n    /// Creates the directory (and parents) if it does not exist.\n    pub fn with_dir(log_dir: &Path, worker_id: &str, session_id: &str) -> Result<Self> {\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(log_dir)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create log directory: {}\", log_dir.display()))?;\n        let filename = format!(\"{worker_id}-{session_id}.jsonl\");\n        let path = log_dir.join(filename);\n        let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n            .create(true)\n            .append(true)\n            .open(&path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to open log file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n        Ok(FileSink {\n            path,\n            writer: std::sync::Mutex::new(std::io::BufWriter::new(file)),\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Return the path to the log file.\n    pub fn path(&self) -> &Path {\n        &self.path\n    }\n\n    /// Write a boot event directly to the file, bypassing the normal channel.\n    ///\n    /// This is called immediately after FileSink creation to ensure that even if\n    /// the writer thread fails to start, we have a trace in the JSONL file.\n    /// The event is written synchronously and flushed to disk.\n    fn write_boot_event_direct(\n        &self,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        session_id: &str,\n        version: &str,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        Self::write_boot_event_direct_impl(\n            &self.writer,\n            &self.path,\n            worker_id,\n            session_id,\n            version,\n            std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), // 5 second timeout\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Write a boot event directly to the file with a timeout.\n    ///\n    /// This is a timeout-aware variant that prevents indefinite blocking on hung\n    /// filesystems (e.g., network filesystem issues, stale NFS mounts). If the\n    /// write takes longer than the timeout, it returns an error and the caller\n    /// can decide whether to continue or fail.\n    ///\n    /// The timeout is implemented by spawning a thread to do the blocking I/O\n    /// and joining with a timeout. If the timeout expires, the thread is detached\n    /// and will continue running (and eventually complete or be killed by the OS).\n    fn write_boot_event_direct_impl(\n        _writer: &std::sync::Mutex<std::io::BufWriter<std::fs::File>>,\n        path: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        session_id: &str,\n        version: &str,\n        timeout: std::time::Duration,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        use std::io::Write;\n        use std::sync::mpsc;\n        use std::thread;\n\n        let event = TelemetryEvent {\n            timestamp: Utc::now(),\n            event_type: \"worker.booting\".to_string(),\n            worker_id: worker_id.to_string(),\n            session_id: session_id.to_string(),\n            sequence: 0,\n            bead_id: None,\n            workspace: None,\n            duration_ms: None,\n            data: serde_json::json!({ \"worker_name\": worker_id, \"version\": version }),\n            trace_id: None,\n            span_id: None,\n        };\n        let line = serde_json::to_string(&event)?;\n        let path_for_error = path.display().to_string();\n        let path_clone = path.to_path_buf();\n\n        // JoinHandle::join() has no timeout in Rust's std — use a channel with recv_timeout.\n        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<Result<(), String>>();\n\n        // The JoinHandle is intentionally dropped on timeout: dropping detaches the thread,\n        // which continues running until it completes or the process exits.\n        let _handle = thread::spawn(move || {\n            let result: Result<(), String> = (|| {\n                let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n                    .create(true)\n                    .append(true)\n                    .open(&path_clone)\n                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                let mut writer = std::io::BufWriter::new(file);\n                writeln!(writer, \"{line}\").map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                writer.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                writer.get_ref().sync_all().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                Ok(())\n            })();\n            // Ignore send error: receiver may have timed out and been dropped.\n            let _ = tx.send(result);\n        });\n\n        match rx.recv_timeout(timeout) {\n            Ok(Ok(())) => Ok(()),\n            Ok(Err(e)) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"failed to write boot event to {}: {}\",\n                path_for_error,\n                e\n            )),\n            Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"timed out writing boot event to {} after {:?} (filesystem may be hung)\",\n                path_for_error,\n                timeout\n            )),\n            Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"boot event writer thread disconnected for {}\",\n                path_for_error\n            )),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Sink for FileSink {\n    fn accept(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n        use std::io::Write;\n        let line = serde_json::to_string(event)?;\n        let mut writer = self\n            .writer\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"lock poisoned: {}\", e))?;\n        writeln!(writer, \"{line}\")?;\n        writer.flush()?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn flush(&self, _deadline: std::time::Duration) -> Result<()> {\n        use std::io::Write;\n        let mut writer = self\n            .writer\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"lock poisoned: {}\", e))?;\n        writer.flush()?;\n        // fsync to ensure durability before shutdown\n        writer.get_ref().sync_all()?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── StdoutSink ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Human-readable, color-coded telemetry sink for interactive monitoring.\n///\n/// Format: `HH:MM:SS [worker] EVENT detail`\npub struct StdoutSink {\n    format: StdoutFormat,\n    use_color: bool,\n}\n\nimpl StdoutSink {\n    /// Create a new stdout sink from config.\n    pub fn new(config: &StdoutSinkConfig) -> Self {\n        let use_color = match config.color {\n            ColorMode::Always => true,\n            ColorMode::Never => false,\n            ColorMode::Auto => atty_stdout(),\n        };\n        StdoutSink {\n            format: config.format,\n            use_color,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Format a single event as a human-readable line.\n    pub fn format_event(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> String {\n        let time = event.timestamp.format(\"%H:%M:%S\");\n        let worker = &event.worker_id;\n        let etype = &event.event_type;\n\n        // Build the summary portion based on event type.\n        let summary = self.event_summary(event);\n\n        match self.format {\n            StdoutFormat::Minimal => {\n                let line = format!(\"{time} [{worker}] {etype}\");\n                if self.use_color {\n                    self.colorize(etype, &line)\n                } else {\n                    line\n                }\n            }\n            StdoutFormat::Normal => {\n                let bead_part = event\n                    .bead_id\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .map(|b| format!(\" {}\", b.as_ref()))\n                    .unwrap_or_default();\n                let summary_part = if summary.is_empty() {\n                    String::new()\n                } else {\n                    format!(\" ({summary})\")\n                };\n                let line = format!(\n                    \"{time} [{worker}] {}{bead_part}{summary_part}\",\n                    Self::short_type(etype)\n                );\n                if self.use_color {\n                    self.colorize(etype, &line)\n                } else {\n                    line\n                }\n            }\n            StdoutFormat::Verbose => {\n                let bead_part = event\n                    .bead_id\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .map(|b| format!(\" {}\", b.as_ref()))\n                    .unwrap_or_default();\n                let data_part = if event.data.is_object()\n                    && event.data.as_object().map_or(true, |m| m.is_empty())\n                {\n                    String::new()\n                } else {\n                    format!(\" {}\", event.data)\n                };\n                let dur = event\n                    .duration_ms\n                    .map(|d| format!(\" {}ms\", d))\n                    .unwrap_or_default();\n                let line = format!(\n                    \"{time} [{worker}] {}{bead_part}{dur}{data_part}\",\n                    Self::short_type(etype)\n                );\n                if self.use_color {\n                    self.colorize(etype, &line)\n                } else {\n                    line\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Shorten the dotted event type to an uppercase action word.\n    fn short_type(event_type: &str) -> &str {\n        match event_type {\n            \"worker.started\" => \"STARTED\",\n            \"worker.stopped\" => \"STOPPED\",\n            \"worker.errored\" => \"ERROR\",\n            \"worker.exhausted\" => \"EXHAUSTED\",\n            \"worker.idle\" => \"IDLE\",\n            \"worker.state_transition\" => \"STATE\",\n            \"worker.queue_empty\" => \"QUEUE_EMPTY\",\n            \"strand.evaluated\" => \"STRAND\",\n            \"strand.skipped\" => \"STRAND_SKIP\",\n            \"bead.claim.attempted\" => \"CLAIMING\",\n            \"bead.claim.succeeded\" => \"CLAIMED\",\n            \"bead.claim.race_lost\" => \"RACE_LOST\",\n            \"bead.claim.race_lost_skipped\" => \"RACE_LOST_SKIP\",\n            \"bead.claim.failed\" => \"CLAIM_FAIL\",\n            \"bead.released\" => \"RELEASED\",\n            \"bead.completed\" => \"COMPLETED\",\n            \"bead.orphaned\" => \"ORPHANED\",\n            \"agent.dispatched\" => \"DISPATCHED\",\n            \"agent.completed\" => \"AGENT_DONE\",\n            \"outcome.classified\" => \"OUTCOME\",\n            \"outcome.handled\" => \"HANDLED\",\n            \"heartbeat.emitted\" => \"HEARTBEAT\",\n            \"peer.stale\" => \"PEER_STALE\",\n            \"peer.crashed\" => \"PEER_CRASHED\",\n            \"health.check\" => \"HEALTH\",\n            \"effort.recorded\" => \"EFFORT\",\n            \"budget.warning\" => \"BUDGET_WARN\",\n            \"budget.stop\" => \"BUDGET_STOP\",\n            \"rate_limit.wait\" => \"RATE_WAIT\",\n            \"rate_limit.allowed\" => \"RATE_OK\",\n            \"bead.mitosis.evaluated\" => \"MITOSIS_EVAL\",\n            \"bead.mitosis.split\" => \"MITOSIS\",\n            \"bead.mitosis.skipped\" => \"MITOSIS_SKIP\",\n            \"mend.orphaned_lock_removed\" => \"MEND_LOCK\",\n            \"mend.dependency_cleaned\" => \"MEND_DEP\",\n            \"mend.db_repaired\" => \"MEND_REPAIR\",\n            \"mend.db_rebuilt\" => \"MEND_REBUILD\",\n            \"mend.cycle_summary\" => \"MEND_DONE\",\n            \"telemetry.sink_error\" => \"SINK_ERR\",\n            other => other,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Produce a brief summary from the event data.\n    fn event_summary(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> String {\n        let d = &event.data;\n        match event.event_type.as_str() {\n            \"bead.claim.succeeded\" => String::new(),\n            \"agent.dispatched\" => d[\"agent\"]\n                .as_str()\n                .map(|a| format!(\"agent={a}\"))\n                .unwrap_or_default(),\n            \"agent.completed\" => {\n                let exit = d[\"exit_code\"].as_i64().unwrap_or(-1);\n                let dur = d[\"duration_ms\"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);\n                format!(\"exit={exit}, {}\", format_duration_ms(dur))\n            }\n            \"outcome.classified\" => d[\"outcome\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"unknown\").to_string(),\n            \"outcome.handled\" => {\n                let outcome = d[\"outcome\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"?\");\n                let action = d[\"action\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"?\");\n                format!(\"{outcome} → {action}\")\n            }\n            \"effort.recorded\" => {\n                let agent = d[\"agent_name\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"?\");\n                let cost = d[\"estimated_cost_usd\"].as_f64();\n                let dur = d[\"elapsed_ms\"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);\n                match cost {\n                    Some(c) => format!(\"{agent}, {}, ${c:.4}\", format_duration_ms(dur)),\n                    None => format!(\"{agent}, {}\", format_duration_ms(dur)),\n                }\n            }\n            \"worker.stopped\" => {\n                let reason = d[\"reason\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"?\");\n                let beads = d[\"beads_processed\"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);\n                format!(\"{reason}, {beads} beads\")\n            }\n            \"worker.errored\" => d[\"error_message\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"unknown\").to_string(),\n            \"worker.idle\" => {\n                let secs = d[\"backoff_seconds\"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);\n                format!(\"backoff {secs}s\")\n            }\n            \"bead.mitosis.split\" => {\n                let created = d[\"children_created\"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);\n                format!(\"{created} children\")\n            }\n            \"budget.warning\" | \"budget.stop\" => {\n                let cost = d[\"daily_cost\"].as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);\n                let thresh = d[\"threshold\"].as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);\n                format!(\"${cost:.2}/${thresh:.2}\")\n            }\n            _ => String::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Wrap text in ANSI color based on event type category.\n    fn colorize(&self, event_type: &str, text: &str) -> String {\n        let code = match event_type {\n            t if t.starts_with(\"worker.errored\") || t.starts_with(\"budget.stop\") => \"\\x1b[31m\", // red\n            t if t.starts_with(\"bead.claim.succeeded\") || t == \"bead.completed\" => \"\\x1b[32m\", // green\n            t if t.starts_with(\"agent.\") || t.starts_with(\"outcome.\") => \"\\x1b[36m\", // cyan\n            t if t.starts_with(\"bead.claim.race_lost\")\n                || t.starts_with(\"bead.claim.race_lost_skipped\")\n                || t.starts_with(\"bead.claim.failed\")\n                || t == \"bead.released\"\n                || t == \"bead.orphaned\" =>\n            {\n                \"\\x1b[33m\"\n            } // yellow\n            t if t.starts_with(\"budget.warning\") || t.starts_with(\"rate_limit\") => \"\\x1b[33m\", // yellow\n            t if t.starts_with(\"mend.\") || t.starts_with(\"peer.\") => \"\\x1b[35m\", // magenta\n            t if t.starts_with(\"heartbeat\") || t.starts_with(\"health\") => \"\\x1b[90m\", // dim\n            _ => \"\\x1b[0m\",                                                      // reset / default\n        };\n        format!(\"{code}{text}\\x1b[0m\")\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Sink for StdoutSink {\n    fn accept(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n        let line = self.format_event(event);\n        println!(\"{line}\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn flush(&self, _deadline: std::time::Duration) -> Result<()> {\n        use std::io::Write;\n        std::io::stdout().flush()?;\n        Ok(())\n Lower number = higher priority (P1 > P2 > P3).\npub type Priority = u8;\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// BeadStatus\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Lifecycle status of a bead in the store.\n#[non_exhaustive]\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\npub enum BeadStatus {\n    Open,\n    InProgress,\n    Done,\n    /// `br show --json` emits `\"closed\"` for done beads. Treat as equivalent\n    /// to `Done` so deserialization succeeds.\n    Closed,\n    Blocked,\n}\n\nimpl BeadStatus {\n    /// Returns true if the bead is finished (either `Done` or `Closed`).\n    pub fn is_done(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, BeadStatus::Done | BeadStatus::Closed)\n    }\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for BeadStatus {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            BeadStatus::Open => write!(f, \"open\"),\n            BeadStatus::InProgress => write!(f, \"in_progress\"),\n            BeadStatus::Done | BeadStatus::Closed => write!(f, \"done\"),\n            BeadStatus::Blocked => write!(f, \"blocked\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// WorkerState\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Worker finite-state-machine states.\n///\n/// Every state has defined entry conditions, actions, and exit transitions.\n/// There are no implicit states or fallthrough paths.\n#[non_exhaustive]\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(rename_all = \"SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE\")]\npub enum WorkerState {\n    Booting,\n    Selecting,\n    Claiming,\n    Retrying,\n    Building,\n    Dispatching,\n    Executing,\n    Handling,\n    Logging,\n    /// All strands returned empty — worker has nothing to do.\n    Exhausted,\n    /// Received graceful shutdown signal.\n    Stopped,\n    /// Unrecoverable error.\n    Errored,\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for WorkerState {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        let s = match self {\n            WorkerState::Booting => \"BOOTING\",\n            WorkerState::Selecting => \"SELECTING\",\n            WorkerState::Claiming => \"CLAIMING\",\n            WorkerState::Retrying => \"RETRYING\",\n            WorkerState::Building => \"BUILDING\",\n            WorkerState::Dispatching => \"DISPATCHING\",\n            WorkerState::Executing => \"EXECUTING\",\n            WorkerState::Handling => \"HANDLING\",\n            WorkerState::Logging => \"LOGGING\",\n            WorkerState::Exhausted => \"EXHAUSTED\",\n            WorkerState::Stopped => \"STOPPED\",\n            WorkerState::Errored => \"ERRORED\",\n        };\n        f.write_str(s)\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Outcome\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// The classified outcome of an agent process.\n///\n/// Every exit code maps to exactly one variant via `Outcome::classify()`.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum Outcome {\n    /// Exit 0 — agent completed work successfully.\n    Success,\n    /// Non-zero exit indicating a failure (includes app errors 1-99 and unknown codes).\n    Failure,\n    /// Exit 100 or 124 — timeout wrapper expired.\n    Timeout,\n    /// Exit 126 or 127 — agent binary not found or not executable.\n    AgentNotFound,\n    /// Exit 130 (SIGINT) or 143 (SIGTERM) — agent was interrupted.\n    Interrupted,\n    /// Negative exit code — process crashed or was killed by a signal.\n    Crash(i32),\n}\n\nimpl Outcome {\n    /// Classify an exit code into an `Outcome` variant.\n    ///\n    /// Every exit code range has an explicit match arm — no wildcards.\n    /// The `was_interrupted` flag takes precedence over exit code analysis.\n    ///\n    /// # Mapping (per spec)\n    /// - `was_interrupted=true` → `Interrupted` (checked first)\n    /// - exit 0 → `Success`\n    /// - exit 1 → `Failure`\n    /// - exit 124 → `Timeout`\n    /// - exit 127 → `AgentNotFound`\n    /// - exit >128 → `Crash(exit_code)`\n    /// - exit <0 → `Crash(exit_code)`\n    /// - all other → `Failure`\n    pub fn classify(exit_code: i32, was_interrupted: bool) -> Self {\n        // Interrupted flag takes precedence (graceful shutdown path).\n        if was_interrupted {\n            return Outcome::Interrupted;\n        }\n\n        // Explicit mapping for every exit code range — NO wildcards.\n        // Each range is explicitly enumerated to ensure compile errors\n        // if a new Outcome variant is added without updating this match.\n        match exit_code {\n            // Success\n            0 => Outcome::Success,\n            // Explicit failure code\n            1 => Outcome::Failure,\n            // Timeout (GNU timeout exit code)\n            124 => Outcome::Timeout,\n            // Agent not found (shell exit code for missing command)\n            127 => Outcome::AgentNotFound,\n            // Failure range: 2-123\n            2..=123 => Outcome::Failure,\n            // Failure: 125-128 (not >128 per spec, so 128 is not Crash)\n            125..=128 => Outcome::Failure,\n            // Signal exits: >128 (128 + signal number)\n            // These are all crashes per the spec.\n            129..=i32::MAX => Outcome::Crash(exit_code),\n            // Negative exit codes (abnormal termination)\n            i32::MIN..=-1 => Outcome::Crash(exit_code),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Return a string representation for telemetry/logging.\n    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            Outcome::Success => \"success\",\n            Outcome::Failure => \"failure\",\n            Outcome::Timeout => \"timeout\",\n            Outcome::AgentNotFound => \"agent_not_found\",\n            Outcome::Interrupted => \"interrupted\",\n            Outcome::Crash(_) => \"crash\",\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// StrandError / StrandResult\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Error returned by a strand evaluation.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum StrandError {\n    StoreError(anyhow::Error),\n    ConfigError(String),\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for StrandError {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            StrandError::StoreError(e) => write!(f, \"bead store error: {}\", e),\n            StrandError::ConfigError(s) => write!(f, \"strand configuration error: {}\", s),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::error::Error for StrandError {\n    fn source(&self) -> Option<&(dyn std::error::Error + 'static)> {\n        match self {\n            StrandError::StoreError(e) => Some(e.as_ref()),\n            StrandError::ConfigError(_) => None,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Result of a strand evaluation in the waterfall.\n#[non_exhaustive]\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum StrandResult {\n    /// One or more candidate beads were found.\n    BeadFound(Vec<Bead>),\n    /// The strand synthesized new work (e.g., mitosis created child beads).\n    WorkCreated,\n    /// This strand found nothing; continue to the next strand.\n    NoWork,\n    /// The strand encountered an error during evaluation.\n    Error(StrandError),\n    /// A bead with too many consecutive failures should be split.\n    ///\n    /// Contains the bead to split and the current failure count.\n    Split(Bead, u32),\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// ClaimResult / ClaimOutcome\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Result of a single claim attempt for one bead.\n#[non_exhaustive]\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum ClaimResult {\n    /// This worker successfully claimed the bead.\n    Claimed(Bead),\n    /// Another worker claimed the bead first.\n    RaceLost {\n        /// Assignee that won the race.\n        claimed_by: String,\n    },\n    /// The bead cannot be claimed (not open, blocked, etc.).\n    NotClaimable {\n        /// Human-readable reason.\n        reason: String,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Aggregate outcome after exhausting all candidates for a selection cycle.\n#[non_exhaustive]\n#[derive(Debug)]\n#[allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]\npub enum ClaimOutcome {\n    /// Successfully claimed a bead.\n    Claimed(Bead),\n    /// Raced every candidate and lost every time.\n    AllRaceLost,\n    /// The strand returned no candidates.\n    NoCandidates,\n    /// The bead store returned an error.\n    StoreError(anyhow::Error),\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// BrDependency\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A bead dependency as returned from the `br` JSON output.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct BrDependency {\n    pub id: BeadId,\n    pub title: String,\n    pub status: String,\n    pub priority: Priority,\n    pub dependency_type: String,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Bead struct\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Serde helper: treats an empty JSON string as `None`.\nfn empty_string_as_none<'de, D>(d: D) -> Result<Option<String>, D::Error>\nwhere\n    D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,\n{\n    let s = Option::<String>::deserialize(d)?;\n    Ok(s.filter(|s| !s.is_empty()))\n}\n\n/// A bead as returned from the bead store.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Bead {\n    pub id: BeadId,\n    pub title: String,\n    /// Stored as `description` in br JSON output.\n    #[serde(rename = \"description\")]\n    pub body: Option<String>,\n    pub priority: Priority,\n    pub status: BeadStatus,\n    /// br emits `\"\"` for unassigned beads; normalize to `None`.\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"empty_string_as_none\")]\n    pub assignee: Option<String>,\n    /// br may omit this field when empty.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub labels: Vec<String>,\n    /// Stored as `source_repo` in br JSON output.\n    #[serde(rename = \"source_repo\", default)]\n    pub workspace: std::path::PathBuf,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub dependencies: Vec<BrDependency>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub dependents: Vec<BrDependency>,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    // br ready --json omits updated_at; default to now so explore can deserialize it\n    #[serde(default = \"chrono::Utc::now\")]\n    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// AgentOutcome\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Raw output from an agent process (before outcome classification).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct AgentOutcome {\n    pub exit_code: i32,\n    pub stdout: String,\n    pub stderr: String,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// NeedleError\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Top-level NEEDLE error type.\n///\n/// Tier is encoded in the variant, so recovery routing is type-driven.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum NeedleError {\n    /// Transient: retry after backoff (network hiccup, lock contention).\n    Transient {\n        message: String,\n        bead_id: Option<BeadId>,\n    },\n    /// Bead-scoped: abandon this bead; other beads can proceed.\n    BeadScoped { message: String, bead_id: BeadId },\n    /// Worker-scoped: this worker should shut down; fleet continues.\n    WorkerScoped {\n        message: String,\n        worker_id: WorkerId,\n    },\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for NeedleError {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            NeedleError::Transient {\n                message,\n                bead_id: Some(id),\n            } => {\n                write!(f, \"transient error (bead {}): {}\", id, message)\n            }\n            NeedleError::Transient {\n                message,\n                bead_id: None,\n            } => {\n                write!(f, \"transient error: {}\", message)\n            }\n            NeedleError::BeadScoped { message, bead_id } => {\n                write!(f, \"bead-scoped error (bead {}): {}\", bead_id, message)\n            }\n            NeedleError::WorkerScoped { message, worker_id } => {\n                write!(f, \"worker-scoped error (worker {}): {}\", worker_id, message)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::error::Error for NeedleError {}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// InputMethod\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// How the prompt is passed to the agent binary.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"method\", rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\npub enum InputMethod {\n    /// Write the prompt to the agent's stdin.\n    Stdin,\n    /// Write the prompt to a temp file and pass the path.\n    File {\n        /// Template for the temp file path. `{bead_id}` is substituted.\n        path_template: String,\n    },\n    /// Pass the prompt as a CLI argument.\n    Args {\n        /// Flag name (e.g., `--prompt`).\n        flag: String,\n    },\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HeartbeatFile / PeerStatus\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Path reference to a worker's heartbeat file on disk.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct HeartbeatFile {\n    pub path: std::path::PathBuf,\n}\n\n/// Health status of a peer worker as inferred from its heartbeat file.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum PeerStatus {\n    /// Heartbeat is fresh — peer is considered alive.\n    Alive {\n        last_seen: DateTime<Utc>,\n        current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n    },\n    /// Heartbeat TTL has elapsed — peer may be stuck.\n    Stale {\n        last_seen: DateTime<Utc>,\n        claimed_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n    },\n    /// Heartbeat file is missing — peer is dead or never started.\n    Dead { heartbeat_file: HeartbeatFile },\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn bead_id_roundtrip() {\n        // From<String>\n        let id = BeadId::from(\"needle-gob\".to_string());\n        // Display\n        assert_eq!(id.to_string(), \"needle-gob\");\n        // FromStr\n        let parsed: BeadId = \"needle-gob\".parse().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(id, parsed);\n        // From<BeadId> for String\n        let s: String = id.clone().into();\n        assert_eq!(s, \"needle-gob\");\n        // From<&str>\n        let id2 = BeadId::from(\"needle-gob\");\n        assert_eq!(id, id2);\n        // Deref\n        assert_eq!(&*id, \"needle-gob\");\n        // AsRef<str>\n        let _: &str = id.as_ref();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn worker_state_serialization() {\n        // Verify SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE serialization\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&WorkerState::Booting).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"BOOTING\"\"#);\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&WorkerState::Selecting).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"SELECTING\"\"#);\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&WorkerState::Exhausted).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"EXHAUSTED\"\"#);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn bead_status_serialization() {\n        // Verify snake_case serialization\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&BeadStatus::Open).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"open\"\"#);\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&BeadStatus::InProgress).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"in_progress\"\"#);\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&BeadStatus::Done).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"done\"\"#);\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&BeadStatus::Blocked).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(json, r#\"\"blocked\"\"#);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn outcome_classify_key_codes() {\n        // Core mappings per spec\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(0, false), Outcome::Success);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(1, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(124, false), Outcome::Timeout);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(127, false), Outcome::AgentNotFound);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn outcome_classify_ranges() {\n        // 2..=123 map to Failure (except 124 which is Timeout)\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(2, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(50, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(99, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(100, false), Outcome::Failure); // NOT timeout per spec\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(123, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        // 125-126 -> Failure (not AgentNotFound per spec)\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(125, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(126, false), Outcome::Failure);\n        // >128 -> Crash (signal exits)\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(128, false), Outcome::Failure); // 128 is NOT >128 per spec\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(129, false), Outcome::Crash(129));\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(130, false), Outcome::Crash(130)); // SIGINT -> Crash\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(137, false), Outcome::Crash(137)); // SIGKILL\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(143, false), Outcome::Crash(143)); // SIGTERM -> Crash\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(255, false), Outcome::Crash(255));\n        // negative -> Crash\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(-1, false), Outcome::Crash(-1));\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(-9, false), Outcome::Crash(-9));\n        // Large positive values >255 -> Crash per spec (exit > 128)\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(256, false), Outcome::Crash(256));\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(1000, false), Outcome::Crash(1000));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn outcome_classify_interrupted_flag() {\n        // was_interrupted=true always returns Interrupted, regardless of exit code\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(0, true), Outcome::Interrupted);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(1, true), Outcome::Interrupted);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(127, true), Outcome::Interrupted);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(-1, true), Outcome::Interrupted);\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::classify(137, true), Outcome::Interrupted);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn outcome_as_str() {\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::Success.as_str(), \"success\");\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::Failure.as_str(), \"failure\");\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::Timeout.as_str(), \"timeout\");\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::AgentNotFound.as_str(), \"agent_not_found\");\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::Interrupted.as_str(), \"interrupted\");\n        assert_eq!(Outcome::Crash(137).as_str(), \"crash\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn }\"#;\n        let bead: Bead = serde_json::from_str(json).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(bead.id, BeadId::from(\"needle-abc\"));\n        assert_eq!(bead.title, \"Test bead\");\n #[test]\n    fn needle_error_display() {\n        let e = NeedleError::Transient {\n            message: \"connection refused\".to_string(),\n            bead_id: Some(BeadId::from(\"needle-xyz\")),\n        };\n        let s = e.to_string();\n        assert!(s.contains(\"needle-xyz\"), \"expected bead id in: {}\", s);\n        assert!(\n            s.contains(\"connection refused\"),\n  #[test]\n    fn bead_status_display() {\n        assert_eq!(BeadStatus::Open.to_string(), \"open\");\n        assert_eq!(BeadStatus::InProgress.to_string(), \"in_progress\");\n {\n        assert_eq!(WorkerState::Booting.to_string(), \"BOOTING\");\n        assert_eq!(WorkerState::Selecting.to_string(), \"SELECTING\");\n        assert_eq!(WorkerState::Claiming.to_string(), \"CLAIMING\");\n        assert_eq!(WorkerState::Retrying.to_string(), \"RETRYING\");\n        assert_eq!(WorkerState::Building.to_string(), \"BUILDING\");\n BeadAction::None => write!(f, \"none\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HandlerResult\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Result of handling an agent outcome.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct HandlerResult {\n    /// The classified
