prevents false-positive starvation alerts by\n/// distinguishing between \"queue genuinely empty\" vs \"all work claimed by\n/// other workers\" vs \"beads exist but are invisible due to configuration.\"\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\npub enum ExhaustionDiagnosis {\n    /// No beads exist in the workspace at all Pluck strand: primary bead selection from the assigned workspace.\n//!\n//! Pluck handles >90% of all bead processing. It queries the bead store for\n//! unassigned, ready beads, filters by excluded labels, and sorts them in\n//! deterministic priority order: `(priority ASC, created_at ASC, id ASC)`.\n//!\n//! Given the same queue state, every worker computes the same candidate list.\n\nuse crate::bead_store::{BeadStore, Filters};\nuse crate::types::{Bead, StrandError, StrandResult};\n\n/// Default labels excluded from Pluck selection when not configured.\nconst DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_LABELS: &[&str] = &[\"deferred\", \"human\", \"blocked\"];\n\n/// The Pluck strand — primary work selection.\npub struct PluckStrand {\n    /// Labels to exclude from candidate selection.\n    exclude_labels: Vec<String>,\n    /// Auto-split beads after this many consecutive failures (0 = disabled).\n    split_after_failures: u32,\n}\n\nimpl PluckStrand {\n    /// Create a new PluckStrand with the given exclude labels.\n    ///\n    /// If `exclude_labels` is empty, the default set (`deferred`, `human`,\n    /// `blocked`) is used.\n    pub fn new(exclude_labels: Vec<String>) -> Self {\n        let labels = if exclude_labels.is_empty() {\n            DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_LABELS\n                .iter()\n                .map(|s| (*s).to_string())\n                .collect()\n        } else {\n            exclude_labels\n        };\n        PluckStrand {\n            exclude_labels: labels,\n            split_after_failures: 3, // default threshold\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a new PluckStrand with the given exclude labels and split threshold.\n    ///\n    /// If `exclude_labels` is empty, the default set (`deferred`, `human`,\n    /// `blocked`) is used.\n    pub fn with_split_threshold(exclude_labels: Vec<String>, split_after_failures: u32) -> Self {\n        let labels = if exclude_labels.is_empty() {\n            DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_LABELS\n                .iter()\n                .map(|s| (*s).to_string())\n                .collect()\n        } else {\n            exclude_labels\n        };\n        PluckStrand {\n            exclude_labels: labels,\n            split_after_failures,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Extract the failure count from a bead's labels.\n    ///\n    /// Labels follow the pattern `failure-count:N`. Returns the count if found,\n    /// or 0 if no failure-count label exists.\n    fn extract_failure_count(bead: &Bead) -> u32 {\n        bead.labels\n            .iter()\n            .filter_map(|l| l.strip_prefix(\"failure-count:\"))\n            .filter_map(|s| s.parse::<u32>().ok())\n            .max()\n            .unwrap_or(0)\n    }\n\n    /// Sort candidates in deterministic priority order.\n    ///\n    /// Sort key: `(priority ASC, created_at ASC, id ASC)`.\n    /// The id tie-breaker ensures identical ordering across platforms.\n    fn sort_candidates(candidates: &mut [Bead]) {\n        candidates.sort_by(|a, b| {\n            a.priority\n                .cmp(&b.priority)\n                .then_with(|| a.created_at.cmp(&b.created_at))\n                .then_with(|| a.id.as_ref().cmp(b.id.as_ref()))\n        });\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait::async_trait]\nimpl super::Strand for PluckStrand {\n    fn name(&self) -> &str {\n        \"pluck\"\n    }\n\n    #[tracing::instrument(\n        name = \"strand.pluck\",\n        skip(self, store),\n        fields(\n            strand = \"pluck\",\n            exclude_labels = ?self.exclude_labels,\n        )\n    )]\n    async fn evaluate(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore) -> StrandResult {\n        // 1. Query bead store for ready, unassigned beads.\n        let filters = Filters {\n            assignee: None,\n            exclude_labels: self.exclude_labels.clone(),\n        };\n\n        let mut candidates = match store.ready(&filters).await {\n            Ok(beads) => beads,\n            Err(e) => {\n                // Bead store error is semantically different from NoWork.\n                return StrandResult::Error(StrandError::StoreError(e));\n            }\n        };\n\n        // 2. Filter: remove beads with excluded labels.\n        //    Defensive guard — store.ready() passes exclude_labels in its Filters,\n        //    but the backing CLI may not include label data in every query type.\n        //    Filtering here guarantees excluded-label beads are never presented as\n        //    candidates regardless of backend behaviour, preventing the\n        //    SELECTING→CLAIMING→RETRYING spin loop observed when br ready --json\n        //    omits label fields for some beads.\n        candidates.retain(|b| !b.labels.iter().any(|l| self.exclude_labels.contains(l)));\n\n        // 3. Filter: remove beads that are actively in_progress (claimed by another worker)\n        //    and Open beads with a stale assignee. These are never claimable — the claimer\n        //    will reject them every time, causing a hot loop.\n        candidates.retain(|b| {\n            !(matches!(b.status, crate::types::BeadStatus::InProgress)\n                || (b.status == crate::types::BeadStatus::Open && b.assignee.is_some()))\n        });\n\n        // 4. Sort: deterministic (priority, created_at, id).\n        Self::sort_candidates(&mut candidates);\n\n        // 5. Check for split trigger: if the first candidate has accumulated\n        //    enough consecutive failures, dispatch a SPLIT instruction instead\n        //    of returning the bead for normal processing.\n        if self.split_after_failures > 0 {\n            if let Some(first_candidate) = candidates.first() {\n                let failure_count = Self::extract_failure_count(first_candidate);\n                if failure_count >= self.split_after_failures {\n                    return StrandResult::Split(first_candidate.clone(), failure_count);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // 6. Return result.\n        if candidates.is_empty() {\n            StrandResult::NoWork\n        } else {\n            StrandResult::BeadFound(candidates)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── Unit tests         beads: vec![\n                make_bead(\"low-pri\", 2, \"2026-01-01 00:00:00\"),\n                make_bead(\"high-pri\", 1, \"2026-01-02 00:00:00\"),\n   /// A restart cap prevents infinite loops (e.g. a strand that always creates\n    /// work without producing a claimable bead).\n    pub async fn select(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn (strand_results::ERROR.to_string(), true),\n                    StrandResult::Split(_, _failure_count) => {\n                        (format!(\"{}({})\", strand_results::BEAD_FOUND, 1), true)\n                    }\n                };\n                tracing::Span::current().record(attrs::NEEDLE_STRAND_RESULT, &result_str);\n                tracing::Span::current().record(attrs::NEEDLE_STRAND_DURATION_MS, elapsed_ms);\n\n                // Set strand span status: Error for strand errors, Ok for all other results\n                if matches!(result, StrandResult::Error(_)) {\n                    tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_code\", 2u64);\n                    tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_description\", &result_str);\n                }\n\n                // Only record evaluations that produced meaningful results.\n                // Skip recording empty BeadFound results since they don't\n                // represent actual strand activity.\n                if should_record {\n                    strand_evaluations.push(StrandEvaluation {\n                        strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n                        result: result_str.clone(),\n                        duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n                    });\n                }\n\n                match result {\n                    StrandResult::BeadFound(beads) => {\n                        // Filter out beads that are in the exclusion set (e.g.\n                        // recently race-lost).  This prevents the waterfall from\n                        // immediately re-selecting a bead that just lost a claim\n                        // race to another worker.\n                        let original_count = beads.len();\n                        let filtered: Vec<Bead> = beads\n                            .into_iter()\n                            .filter(|b| !exclusions.contains(&b.id))\n                            .collect();\n                        let excluded_count = original_count.saturating_sub(filtered.len());\n\n                        // Record queue depth for the Pluck strand (after filtering).\n                        // This samples the current queue depth for the needle.queue.depth\n                        // observable gauge, which is measured at strand evaluation.\n                        // We report depth per priority level to enable filtered views.\n                        if strand_name == \"pluck\" {\n                            use std::collections::HashMap;\n                            let mut depths: HashMap<u8, u64> = HashMap::new();\n                            for bead in &filtered {\n                                *depths.entry(bead.priority).or_insert(0) += 1;\n                            }\n                            self.telemetry.record_queue_depth(depths);\n                        }\n\n                        if let Err(e) =\n                            self.telemetry\n                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n                                    result: \"bead_found\".to_string(),\n                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n                                })\n                        {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                strand = %strand_name,\n                                error = %e,\n                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n                            );\n                        }\n                        tracing::info!(\n                            strand = %strand_name,\n                            candidates = filtered.len(),\n                            excluded = excluded_count,\n                            elapsed_ms,\n                            \"strand found candidates\"\n                        );\n                        if let Some(bead) = filtered.into_iter().next() {\n                            // Keep the strand span active through the bead lifecycle.\n                            // The span guard will be dropped when the bead lifecycle ends.\n                            return Ok(SelectOutcome {\n                                bead: Some((bead, strand_name.clone())),\n                                waterfall_restarts: restarts,\n                                restart_triggers,\n                                strand_evaluations,\n                                strand_span_guard: Some(strand_enter),\n                                split_failure_count: None,\n                            });\n                        }\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    StrandResult::Split(bead, failure_count) => {\n                        if let Err(e) =\n                            self.telemetry\n                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n                                    result: \"split\".to_string(),\n                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n                                })\n                        {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                strand = %strand_name,\n                                error = %e,\n                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n                            );\n                        }\n                        tracing::info!(\n                            strand = %strand_name,\n                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n                            failure_count,\n                            \"strand triggered split for bead with excessive failures\"\n                        );\n                        return Ok(SelectOutcome {\n                            bead: Some((bead, strand_name.clone())),\n                            waterfall_restarts: restarts,\n                            restart_triggers,\n                            strand_evaluations,\n                            strand_span_guard: Some(strand_enter),\n                            split_failure_count: Some(failure_count),\n                        });\n                    }\n                    StrandResult::WorkCreated => {\n                        if let Err(e) =\n                            self.telemetry\n                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n                                    result: \"work_created\".to_string(),\n                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n                                })\n                        {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                strand = %strand_name,\n                                error = %e,\n                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n                            );\n                        }\n                        restarts += 1;\n                        restart_triggers.push(strand_name.clone());\n                        if restarts > MAX_RESTARTS {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                strand = %strand_name,\n                                max_restarts = MAX_RESTARTS,\n                                \"waterfall restart cap reached, continuing to evaluate remaining strands\"\n                            );\n                            // Do NOT return None — continue evaluating remaining strands\n                            // so every strand emits telemetry and the operator can see\n                            // why the worker is idle.\n                            continue;\n                        }\n                        tracing::info!(\n                            strand = %strand_name,\n                            elapsed_ms,\n                            restart = restarts,\n                            \"strand created new work, restarting waterfall\"\n                        );\n                        continue 'waterfall;\n                    }\n                    StrandResult::NoWork => {\n                        if let Err(e) =\n                            self.telemetry\n                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n                                    result: \"no_work\".to_string(),\n                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n                                })\n                        {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                strand = %strand_name,\n                                error = %e,\n                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n                            );\n                        }\n                        tracing::info!(\n                            strand = %strand_name,\n                            elapsed_ms,\n                            \"strand returned no work\"\n                        );\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    StrandResult::Error(e) => {\n                        if let Err(te) =\n                            self.telemetry\n                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n                                    result: \"error\".to_string(),\n                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n                                })\n                        {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                strand = %strand_name,\n                                error = %te,\n                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n                            );\n                        }\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            strand = %strand_name,\n                            error = %e,\n                            elapsed_ms,\n                            \"strand error, continuing to next strand\"\n                        );\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            // All strands evaluated without finding work or triggering a restart.\n            return Ok(SelectOutcome {\n                bead: None,\n                waterfall_restarts: restarts,\n                restart_triggers,\n                strand_evaluations,\n                strand_span_guard: None,\n                split_failure_count: None,\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Return the names of all configured strands (for telemetry/debugging).\n    pub fn strand_names(&self) -> Vec<&str> {\n        self.strands.iter().map(|s| s.name()).collect()\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── Unit tests ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::types::{Bead,
